HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001453/B001","root_001453/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_18033846992ed14f5a42","text":"2. **The lowly input magnifies the transformation.** `م ه ن:B001` gives lowliness and weakness; `م ه ن:B006` narrows the image to scant, weak generative fluid. The following ayah assigns completion, life-spirit, hearing, sight, and hearts to what comes from that input. **Inferred arrow:** minimal and low-status material -> highly organized, perceptive human result. *Māʾin mahīn* functions as the low end of a causal asymmetry, making the subsequent endowment more striking rather than degrading the finished human alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000736/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1c209b96b6cd5e07a878","text":"2. **A hidden chain that resists the image of total dispersal.** **س ل ل B005** supplies chainlike connection, while **ن س ل B001** supplies successive procreation. Later speakers imagine themselves disappearing into earth (32:10). I infer that the managed succession here is a model of identity persisting through material transitions. The ayah can therefore activate a linked-continuity reading: what seems like a vanishing drop is already part of a chain capable of renewed embodiment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000736/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_36dbf7fd73e4bd0dfa10","text":"3. **Extraction answers the later fear of disappearance.** The fixed *sulālah* is anchored in `س ل ل:B001/B003`; 32:10 then imagines the person disappearing into earth, and 32:11 answers with complete taking. The packet supplies an earlier successful extraction from inconspicuous material. **Inferred arrow:** one demonstrated drawing-out from a hidden material matrix -> an analogy for recoverability after terrestrial concealment. The reproductive statement acquires a latent resurrection function within its window.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001458/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_39f0514a65bf51a31601","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:8:7\",\"root_001458\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Üreme sıvısının rahme yerleştirilmesi çağrışımı, zayıf başlangıç maddesinin koruyucu bir kuşatma içinde taşındığını düşündürebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000736/B001","root_001499/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_606bf830944550996c56","text":"1. **Lineage is a repeated extraction chain.** `ن س ل:B001` gives progeny born in succession; `س ل ل:B001` gives gentle hidden drawing-out, and `س ل ل:B003` gives extracted lineage, essence, or drop. The nested *min … min* construction places the progeny inside an extraction from fluid, while *thumma* connects this route to the clay beginning in 32:7 and completion in 32:9. **Inferred arrow:** repeated extraction of an essence from an origin -> continuation of progeny through successive embodied links. *Naslahu min sulālatin* thus reads as a process of transmitted derivation, not merely a label for semen.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000736/B001","root_000736/B003","root_001453/B006","root_001458/B004","root_001499/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_864d6d250d60d0b489ea","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:8:3\",\"root_001499\",[\"B001\"]],[\"32:8:5\",\"root_000736\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"32:8:7\",\"root_001458\",[\"B004\"]],[\"32:8:8\",\"root_001453\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İnsan soyu, güçlü bir maddeden değil, zayıf bir sıvıdan özün dikkatle çekilip ardışık bedenlere aktarılmasıyla süren bir çıkarma zinciri olarak görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000736/B005","root_001499/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bb666e96b9b21f781dc7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:8:3\",\"root_001499\",[\"B001\"]],[\"32:8:5\",\"root_000736\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Özün zincir gibi bağlı aktarımı, görünmez hale gelmenin bütünüyle kaybolmak anlamına gelmediğine ve maddi geçişler boyunca sürekliliğin korunabildiğine örnek olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001458/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bbdf85200f24b7e6d074","text":"3. **Generative water inside a merciful enclosure (exploratory).** **م و ه B004** explicitly supplies placement of male fluid in the womb, and the repeated **ر ح م B003** from 32:0 supplies the womb as vessel. The packet does not state the causal arrow \"mercy carries the fluid\"; I infer it from the opening formula and the birth sequence. The weak water is read not as self-sufficient matter but as material preserved within an enclosing process.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001453/B006","root_001499/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d12bb2834c11b7a32868","text":"1. **Lineage as gentle extraction from weakness.** **ن س ل B001** supplies successive progeny, **س ل ل B001, B003** gentle drawing-out and extracted lineage or essence, **م و ه B004** male fluid placed in the womb, and **م ه ن B006** scant weak generative fluid. The nested مِن phrases repeatedly trace an output back through its source. I infer that continuity is achieved through controlled extraction rather than material strength. Human succession thus reads as a precisely drawn lineage carried by what appears weak.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d59092468740d124bcac","text":"The claim of disappearance in earth at 32:10 made the chain branch of **س ل ل** unexpectedly important: continuity can be hidden without being lost. The water driven into cut-off land in 32:27 later enlarged the generative-water image from the body to the landscape.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000736/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fb6dcfe23b239756fbcf","text":"Later 32:10-11 corrected a possible reading of *sulālah* as mere reproductive description.
